# RH no-go catalogue

[Current ledger](rh-current-frontier-map.md) · [Full source reference](rh-frontier-reference-2026-09-12.md#source-line-2865)

**471 named mechanisms.** This catalogue retains every no-go entry from the frozen ledger
and adds source-backed obstructions, grouped for lookup. Each entry separates the closed
result from its strongest survivor and remaining problem. Grouping is navigational;
it does not assert mathematical independence.

“Closed” can mean **impossible under the stated assumptions**, **nonselective**, or
**equivalent to an RH premise rather than an independent producer**. Read the exact result;
these are not interchangeable claims. A killed subroute does not retire its parent route.

Source-audit cutoff: **2026-09-25, `d0f6214ff`**.
Updated records link to committed Lean statements and explicitly marked paper proofs;
unchanged records retain their historical evidence. No builds, Lean checks, tests, lint
or index refresh were run for this update. FD2's full verdict and
RBH's retired architecture remain accessible from
the [retired-route overview](rh-current-frontier-map.md#killed-routes-and-subroutes).
Concurrent uncommitted Lean sources and paper-card revisions remain pending evidence and are not counted.

<a id="no-go-097"></a>

### A

**Closed result**

Fixed source-faithful physical-phase transport is identity; nonidentity has explicit connection defect. Positive Markov feasibility is source sign, and universal feasibility is RH. Source-trace Hankel positivity and canonical residue closability are also exactly RH; finite Euler orbits and higher grades are blind.

**Survivor / still open**

Phase-natural rigidity, barycentric completeness, trace/resolvent identities, moment-germ inversion, and dense finite-Euler orbit classifiers.

[Back to family](#retired-labels) · [Original evidence and surrounding qualifications](rh-frontier-reference-2026-09-12.md#source-no-go-097)

<a id="no-go-180"></a>

### AF

**Closed result**

Copywise convergent abelian class-law certificate exists exactly at centered rate zero; global certificates exist exactly under RH. Under fixed-source absolute continuity, every nonneutral adaptive response forces admission-aperture mass to evacuate to zero. Determinant classifies finite unitary `π₁`; finite-AF operator `K₁` is exactly its rational charge colimit and exactly the completed-UHF circle operator `K₁`. Every nonzero completed charge has a nonzero finite ancestor. The actual charged Xi boundary loop now has a fixed positive uniform error floor: every continuous periodic zero-winding approximant differs from it by at least one common `m>0` somewhere. This excludes sparse approximation subsequences as well as uniform convergence; the positive survivor is the charged loop together with its quantitative separation. The real Deninger apex sharpens the survivor: its transverse logarithmic flow is translation, so every nonzero invariant measure finite on compacts has infinite mass in every apex neighbourhood.

**Survivor / still open**

AF is terminal as a fixed convergent copywise producer. Any successor must use the now-forced singular/infinite-action archimedean cusp or a genuinely global charged aggregate, and still construct a source-to-Xi orientation law. Completion, stabilization, tensor amplification, and finite or UHF `SU` sectors cannot nucleate the missing charge.

<a id="no-go-341"></a>

### HB

**Closed result**

The general-Fuchsian scattering architecture cannot force second-sheet poles onto the Riemann
line: every geometric, causal, Maass--Selberg and positivity input survives finite-area cusped
deformation while resonances move off the symmetry line. The PSL₂(ℤ)-specific repair programme
through Hecke eigenvalues and moments, Rankin--Selberg phases, Kuznetsov residues, Ward
stationarity, Hardy factorization and resonant-state Gram laws was exhausted; each candidate is
an exact receiver, a positive Cauchy/Breit--Wigner model valid throughout `Re ρ < 1`, or an
RH-equivalent premise. HB therefore has no independent producer and is retired as an RH route.

**Survivor / still open**

The full receiver stack survives: Maass--Selberg, height-one source sign, resonant norm and
partner-angle laws, exact Hecke moments, pulse-layer direct/image decomposition and the Peierls
lift defect. Image domination, strong Goursat regularity and `contactField` identification remain
physical transport problems, not an arithmetic zero-selection route.

Evidence: [producer loop](hb-producer-conjecture-loop.md), [Maass--Selberg model](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/EisensteinMaassSelbergLineModel.lean), [resonant spectral kernel](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/EisensteinResonantSpectralKernel.lean), [pulse layer](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/ThetaCuspHawkingBMLiteralCyclicPeierlsPulseLayer.lean).

<a id="no-go-146"></a>

### J

**Closed result**

For `L_m=6 log m`, `m≥25`, bad energy is at least `log m/20` at every Julia scale. No uniform unrenormalized budget exists; off-diagonal cancellation cannot remove unit-diagonal mass; post hoc diagonal subtraction breaks one-node capacity. The residual jet is exactly a two-index Legendre combination. For every `δ>0`, `k≥2`, it equals one explicit oscillatory uniform main term up to `(cosh δ)^(-1/2)(|a|+|b|)·400/(sin φ(4k+3))`; the fixed-rapidity `k^(3/2)` beat and shrinking-rapidity profile `Φ(X)=4√(2/π)(√X cos(4X)-8X√X sin(4X))` are its two proved limits. Mixed two-mode logarithmic determinant response is exactly `tr T(δ₁+δ₂)+tr T(δ₁-δ₂)-tr(Tδ₁ Tδ₂)`. The induced squared-radius response is the discrete second difference of that determinant jet plus the product of the two first radius jets divided by the unperturbed squared radius.

**Survivor / still open**

Route J remains terminal. A principal-relative current defined before good/bad projection would be a changed route. Uniform residual, determinant, and radius bilinear-response laws are positive survivors, but no theorem aggregates them over literal primes, controls a signed current, or lands it at an RH endpoint.

[Back to family](#retired-labels) · [Original evidence and surrounding qualifications](rh-frontier-reference-2026-09-12.md#source-no-go-146)

<a id="no-go-156"></a>

### R

**Closed result**

Intrinsic producer category is empty. Integral Laurent relative-reflection differential, cofinal Tate operator, source Clifford law, and global Gamma base-change square exist before divisor selection. Their determinant is exact formation-completion index. Source-marked finite formation converges, unique coherent negative-tail primitive exists, shell recovery is closable, compact TMD descent holds, and index vanishes exactly on wall; every global version is exactly RH. Three-slot proof-exchange defect, Gamma-six completion cost, and external-tensor completion occupation obey one exact conservation/odds law and have same zero set, so they are one receiver. Bounded ingress/egress cannot carry nonzero constant cofinal anomaly. Positive-grade twisted Möbius recovery has fractional boundary index and cannot be a single-valued nonzero meromorphic completion. Integral trace/supertrace, dyadic frame, dense Lorentz lattice, and quadratic ghost conditions are further exact wall receivers.

**Survivor / still open**

Source-marked stable/Roos class, squarefull Stokes normality, external tensor curvature, marked formation cost, primary conductor, and integral Tate index feed JAS/WBF/CDC/TK/TMD. Any successor must add a source theorem retaining external polarization and proving properness before divisor specialization. Intrinsic regular, bounded-recovery, single-valued meromorphic, internal-energy, diagonal-multiplication, integral-frame-as-premise, compact-open scalar, copywise recurrence, and proof-exchange/Gamma-cost reparameterization producers are terminal.

### CCM-FU/P normalized critical Abel eventual one-sided sign

**Closed result**

Lean gives an exact positive correction between normalized critical Abel source and Route P's ray, bounded by `(20/3)e^(-t/2)`. Fixed-amplitude positive and negative excursions of the Route P ray therefore persist for the Abel source arbitrarily far out. Neither eventual scalar sign can hold for this normalized source.

**Survivor / still open**

The asymptotic source identification is reusable. A signed, non-scalar growing-core energy/covariance payment and terminal control remain open; two-sided oscillation alone supplies no such bound.

Evidence: [Abel–P bridge and oscillation](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/CriticalAbelRoutePAsymptoticBridge.lean).

### CCM fixed positive exponential-clock bounded source

**Closed result**

For any fixed positive exponential clock, the **literal** completed third-grade prime-minus-polar cumulative source is unbounded; the Abel-shift/Hardy-zero pole test is formalized. This does not establish unboundedness of the distinct connected Suzuki clock window, whose second-Abel/polar bridge remains open.

**Survivor / still open**

The shifted Abel identity identifies the divisor singularity a clock must pay. Moving or zero clocks and source-faithful all-cutoff signed cancellation remain possible; fixed-core macroscopic negative pair drops and middle-bridge neutralization show why a growing active core cannot be dismissed.

Evidence: [fixed exponential-clock no-go](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/CCMGuinandWeilFixedExponentialClockNoGo.lean), [connected-clock status](ccm-fu-fixed-clock-pole-obstruction.md).

<a id="no-go-424"></a>

### CCM-FU fixed or undersized old-core shell payment

**Closed result**

The physical inverse-cube mean of the dense dyadic prime shell `(2N,3N]` diverges, even after adjoining any fixed old prime core. For a disjoint moving core `Q`, the explicit head/debt floor persists whenever `480·2^|Q|≤#(2N,3N] primes`. Fixed-core or such undersized-core positive payment cannot uniformly absorb this shell.

**Survivor / still open**

The exact shell floor quantifies capacity required of a moving old core. CCM-FU may still use a sufficiently large core or signed cancellation across old/new walls; neither an all-cutoff payment nor an exhaustive history is excluded.

Evidence: [shell divergence](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/MobiusDivisorDyadicShellDebtGrowth.lean), [fixed-core debt](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/MobiusDivisorShellAnchorDebt.lean), [moving-core cardinality](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/MobiusDivisorShellAnchorComplexity.lean).

### CCM exterior constant-mode adaptive-mass repair

**Closed result**

For every moving exterior threshold/window scale whose product tends to infinity, fixed-error
approximation of the constant mode by a positive-minus-positive exterior cosine measure forces
its total Jordan mass, divided by every fixed power of that scale, to tend to infinity. Changing
the aperture schedule cannot make this universal constant-mode completion polynomially controlled.

**Survivor / still open**

The scale-covariant lower wall is exact. A viable exterior correction must be source-adapted and
nonconstant, and still prove the signed reverse-prime/source-energy inequality; the theorem does
not obstruct arbitrary such signed gauges.

Evidence: [adaptive exterior mass wall](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/CCMActualHeadExteriorCosineAdaptiveMassWall.lean).

<a id="no-go-015"></a>

### CCM-FU fixed weighted or square-root terminal head

**Closed result**

Local exact thresholds already make every fixed head weight fail on an actual cutoff-25 state. Globally, the complete signed divisor source escapes every fixed `O(sqrt M)` envelope arbitrarily far out and on every fixed positive-integer grid. A square-root Abel bound would force impossible Chebyshev-defect continuation and zero propagation. Any fixed-positive-weight monotone completion of a bounded-quotient small-head terminal core would imply this false square-root bound; actual small terminal cores without such a completion occur cofinally.

**Survivor / still open**

Fixed weighted-head descent and a universal square-root final head are terminal. State-dependent successor-envelope selection survives, but must control the original transverse signed cross terms and permit an unavoidable super-square-root final head while retaining the RH-sufficient `O(M)` scale.

### CCM-FU fixed-block prime schedules

**Closed result**

For every fixed number K of late-prime blocks and every fixed diagonal multiplier C, every admissible partition has an endpoint above `C Q_empty(4N)` for all sufficiently large N.

**Survivor / still open**

Exact positive capacity: a block absorbs at most the sum of its endpoint energy square roots; a K-block partition under ceiling B has `T(M)<=2K sqrt(B)`. Actual positive retained mass is superlinear even inside one fixed macroscopic prime window after the fixed core below eight, with a uniform positive square-root lower bound on every singleton fibre. Hence a bounded-energy proof needs growing signed batches or unboundedly many late/earlier-prime interleavings. Lean now constructs actual matched positive/negative batches consuming arbitrarily large linear mass with every selected prefix under one fixed quadratic ceiling. This closes existence of large safe signed batches, but they leave the middle primes untouched; exhaustive growing adaptive order remains open.

**Closed result**

In `H=f^2+aK`, every isolated late-prime block has increment equal to block mass times one invariant slope, and the normalized slope after prime two tends to positive infinity. Every complete one-prime order therefore exceeds every quadratic head-coarea ceiling, regardless of repetitions, interleavings, or nonprime steps. Removing the remaining late-singleton diagonal restores the actual square but leaves corrected action equal to an unavoidable signed-mass term plus nonnegative variance; every complete order again exceeds every quadratic ceiling. Separately, every positive probability weighting has energy at least absolute source mass squared, with equality for absolute-amplitude weights and literal relative cost of order `M`.

**Survivor / still open**

Raw and corrected coarea actions cannot be global Lyapunov functions, and positive normalization cannot erase source size. This does not rule out a quadratic bound for raw successor energy. State-dependent raw-energy ordering with signed transverse cancellation remains live.

### E boundary singleton divergence at fixed receiver order

**Closed result**

The prime and `2p` selected numerator fibres both have nonsummable order-`1/q` mass at boundary exponent `r=1`, with opposite signs. Lean separately bounds each coefficient-bounded selected cofactor fibre with `a≤q` by `6a q^(-r-1)≤6q^(-r)` for fixed `1<r≤2`; the resulting prime sum is absolutely convergent. <a id="no-go-038"></a>

### EDQ all-prime damping/majorant shortcut

For `c>=1/2`, the correction `C(P,c)=product_(p in P)(1+p^(-c))/(1-p^(-c))` lies between `exp(sum p^(-c))` and `exp(5 sum p^(-c))`. With every prime through `N`, `C>=N^(1-c)`; along the proved Route-T contour, `C*exp(-(log N)^(1/20))>=exp(9(log N)^(1/20))` eventually and tends to infinity at zero frequency. Thus the displayed all-prime upper majorant cannot preserve its saving. In the actual Báez--Duarte carrier, the count-damped Möbius source differs from minus `u` times the complete positive prime packet by at most `4*u^2*sqrt(N)`. The selected `u_N=||P_N||/(8*sqrt(N))<=1/2` yields eventual energy at least `N/(C*log(N)^4)` and amplifies the undamped source beyond every fixed factor or logarithmic power, ruling out any uniform damping budget.

**Survivor / still open**

This carrier obstruction is not a lower bound on scalar finite damped sums. Its expected quantitative growth remains unformalized. Separately, the literal all-prime cofactor Dirichlet series has exact Euler-product integral representation, equals the existing Ramaré coefficient on every admitted cutoff, and satisfies the unit logarithmic-zeta boundary invariant `F(sigma)*log|zeta(sigma)| -> 1` at `1+`, although `F(sigma)->0`. This closes boundary scale, not cutoff partial sums or physical covariance. Another exact positive survivor is the prime-mass budget: `5 sum p^(-c)<=v-r` suffices, while any preserved saving forces `sum p^(-c)<=v-r`. Smaller damped prime sets may satisfy it but retain the rough packet and all mixed correlations. A successful Ramaré/EDQ estimate must exploit cancellation discarded by the positive correction majorant.

### PW-B subcritical scalar full-square contact

**Closed result**

At a hypothetical left-half Xi copy, the actual cross-clock forcing mean cannot equal `a(log y)·sourceMean(y)+d` on the upper cusp when `a` is continuous and `|a(t)|≤M exp(σt)` with `σ` strictly below the source growth exponent. Such a full-square scalar contact becomes integrable after forcing normalization, contradicting the formally proved nonzero translated-window charge.

**Survivor / still open**

The charge supplies a sharp growth-grade boundary: any scalar survivor must carry at least the missing cusp grade, or use a genuinely nonlocal/ordered contact. The no-go does not prove oriented CTP incidence, fixed-grade magnitude, outer extinction or RH.

Evidence: [subcritical contact no-go](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/ThetaCuspHawkingBMPWCrossClockSubcriticalContactNoGo.lean), [source charge](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/ThetaCuspHawkingBMPWCrossClockTranslatedTests.lean).

A return holomorphic on all `Re s>0` also cannot equal it there, because zeta has a pole at `s=1`. The latter conclusion is conditional on the physical port satisfying that holomorphy class; this is not formalized for the slab.

Its grade-two logarithmic coefficient is negative, so the model does **not** preserve the literal theta/von Mangoldt coefficient law. Positivity, evenness and reflection alone cannot confine zeros.

**Survivor / still open**

The exact source-transform construction separates generic positivity from coefficient-faithful arithmetic. Any theta route needs a prime-grade or complex-phase law specific to the literal source.

**Closed result**

On paper, the literal theta half-port is nonzero at Xi zeros, but its right-normal modulus imbalance has both signs at arbitrarily high simple critical ordinates (each crossing direction has lower relative density `5/18`). Neither fixed Hermite–Biehler direction holds. A zero-free finite-exponential-type scalar gauge preserving exact symmetrization is trivial. Strict orientation of a smooth corrected half-port forces cancellation of every even source jet at the causal cut, while finite real prime-translation corrections vanish at each Xi zero and leave the raw alternating slope unchanged. These are paper theorems, not Lean closures.

**Survivor / still open**

The all-even-jet cancellation is a sharp necessary local boundary condition. Lean's midpoint two-jet theorem is a line-classifying receiver, not its source proof.

**Closed result**

Nonzero Bessel seams defeat block positivity; pure phases and zero-mean nodal profiles defeat norm-only arguments; pointwise regulator limits lose mass; reciprocal-amplitude resistance has zero limiting capacity. Eisenstein powers refute uniform positive scalar comparison; fixed generator words have level-divergent cost. Scalar deck averaging annihilates the source. Source-attached diagonal inversion sharpens this: total diagonal loss dominates `N` times source density and some channel is strictly lossy at every positive-level source, but that unsigned loss still does not orient the core boundary. Exceptional sources now have exact zero congruence mean, discharging the old mean premise without supplying a gap. Every nonzero finite complete quarter packet fails native `L²`, while the admitted infinite damped resummation has nonzero Gamma mean and therefore explicit nonzero covariance defect. The zero-mean theta source has strictly positive shifted phase current for every Xi copy, hence is nonselective. The reflected theta field has nonzero forcing and response vectors and exact unit-normalized uncentered/centered graph equations, but both retain one fixed forcing term; inhomogeneous graph admission is not homogeneous eigen-occurrence. Its exact real-line density `Xi(t)^2*w(t)` is nonnegative, yet the Cauchy denominator is complex for off-axis spectral points, so positivity alone supplies no reality law. Complete positive Eisenstein probes prove finite packet separation even at real energies, but give no cofinal quantitative lower bound. Polynomial Casimir and native-scattering shortcuts remain terminal, and the literal corrected source/cutoff family still has positive residual.

**Survivor / still open**

At levels at least eight, current density, exact core--cusp stress laws, and the strict period-product floor survive with the flat-node and unoriented-boundary alternatives. Above the explicit Bessel transition cutoff, every seam is nonnegative, so an exceptional source would force the literal compact-core quarter form to be strictly negative. Prove a source-attached lower bound excluding that negativity, or pairing reality/vanishing, rank-seven/all-order signed positivity and a cofinal residual estimate. Local loss, mean zero, forcing, finite separation and transform identities are closed and cannot substitute for this signed global coercivity.

is dense. Compactness must be proved on this restricted cuspidal carrier, not on whole deck `L²`.

<a id="no-go-242"></a>

### finite, differential, modulus/Newton, power-profield/Jensen, inertia, orbit, deformation, fixed-dimension passports, or squarefree-half regularization

**Closed result**

Exact-order-one Schwarz-real families preserve finite jets, power-translation fields, divisibility towers, maximal translation differential independence, and finite Pick/Krein data while inserting off-real quartets. Exact seam inertia only counts supplied off-line pairs. Pole-cleared compact-open square approximation on `rightHalf` is already `RHLadder 1`. Canonical convolution roots are automatic but not absolutely summable for `Re s <= 1`; raw half partial sums and every cofinal Cayley schedule are unbounded. Fixed-anchor point-Hermite correction is terminal for every bounded, unbounded, or oscillatory depth schedule. Interior-clustered moving anchors are terminal; noncompact escape is topologically sharp and survives functional-equation, Schwarz, hypertranscendence, and finite-passport constraints. Every cofinal Cesàro pole schedule is terminal. **Every positive Laplace heat clock tending to zero with a cofinal pole-degree clock is terminal**, including arbitrarily sparse schedules, for this literal squarefree-half family.

The complete signed heat source diverges at `s=2/3`; the actual pole factor has norm
at least `1/2` at every degree at least four. Hence the normalized two-clock family
cannot be locally bounded on `rightHalf`, with no density, mesh, Kato or RH premise.

**Survivor / still open**

Finite passports, coefficient magnitudes, Newton/profield/differential/Jensen data, deformation continuity, finite inertia, raw Cayley schedules, fixed-anchor Hermite correction, interior-clustered anchors, all Cesàro schedules, and every positive cofinal Laplace heat clock cannot orient Xi through these literal families. The [all-clock source-mass theorem](../5-failed/dyadic-root-laplace-source-mass.md) closes sparse rescheduling and retains a strictly positive fraction of the complete signed source mass. A source-sensitive noncompact/distributed or genuinely compensated continuation remains outside these no-gos. Selberg--Delange continuation reduces separately to the undilated `ZetaBinomialHalfKatoInversion`; all fixed dilations and the summable moving-Tannery majorant follow formally. Gamma-half scaling and the now-committed harmonic-window law are joined by the actual matched local Kato boundary germ and localized Fourier decay. The final Tauberian inversion and sharp ordinary coefficient sum remain unproved. This is a candidate interface inside squarefree-half continuation, not a route label.

Current evidence: [all-clock theorem](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/SquarefreeConvolutionHalfLaplaceAllClocksNoGo.lean), [positive source-mass reserve](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/SquarefreeConvolutionHalfHeatMass.lean), [uniform pole-factor floor](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/PoleFactorTwoThirdsLowerBound.lean). The failed-route card records the survivor's paper/Lean status; this update performs no fresh proof check.

The Q observable is the oblique projection of the completed prime--Gamma flux, not its real part.
The explicit flux value `-i` has nonnegative real component but the wrong Q projection. Therefore
a D2 lower bound on real flux alone cannot imply the Q exterior sign.

**Survivor / still open**

The exact oblique-flux identity and the new order-23 positive exterior-overlap band survive. A
cofinal proof must control the full complex phase, or prove a source law coupling real and
imaginary flux, uniformly across the upper strip.

Evidence: [D2/Q oblique-flux identity and counterexample](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/PrimeArchD2QObliqueFlux.lean).

<a id="no-go-454"></a>

### Mass-only bounds for a unit-height off-line Poisson bump

**Closed result**

Lean proves an elementary bump `d²/(d²+(t−γ)²)` has peak one at `t=γ`, while its integral on any symmetric finite window is `<π|d|`. For arbitrarily small nonzero displacement, every fixed amplification of this mass is arbitrarily small although the peak persists. Thus no **uniform bound on bump mass alone** controls the height needed by the critical-line Poisson receiver. This is a kernel countermodel, not a theorem that every zero-density method fails.

**Survivor / still open**

The explicit scale `|d|` and unit-height threshold identify the needed pointwise/resolution-sensitive quantity. Prove `U(t)<1` for all heights from literal arithmetic, or derive another source-side law; the Lean equivalence itself is only an RH receiver.

Evidence: [bump integral and countermodel](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/DisplacementMassBlindness.lean), [Poisson receiver](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/CriticalLinePoissonPotential.lean).

<a id="no-go-063"></a>

### convolution-inverse analytic twins

**Closed result**

If coefficient streams satisfy `a*d=1`, both `L`-series converge on the Euler chamber, and both admit holomorphic continuations to `Re s>1/2`, their product is one first on the Euler chamber and then everywhere by analytic uniqueness. Both continuations are zero-free.

**Survivor / still open**

Convolution-inverse twins cannot carry or split the Xi divisor. A genuine twin must multiply to Xi, reducing by twin rigidity to a right-half square root times an analytic unit.

**Closed result**

Every finite-particle analytic right cocycle on the stated convex symmetric domain, identity on generating collisions, is globally holomorphically gauge-trivial. Scalar CDD pair twists do not alter Hardy intertwining. A regular nondegenerate difference-form braid family obeys Sutherland linearization and has projectively differential-algebraic entries, so it cannot encode a hypertranscendental clock pair by analytic reparametrization. The explicit non-difference model has a nontrivial unipotent specialization with no positive one-particle unitarizing metric.

**Survivor / still open**

Genuinely non-difference, singular/degenerate, infinite-particle, or independent arithmetic incidence with coefficient-faithful Xi landing.

<a id="no-go-061"></a>

### fixed-domain same-fibre holomorphic Kramers family

**Closed result**

In arbitrary complex inner-product dimension, weak holomorphy plus commutation with one fixed antiunitary makes every matrix coefficient occur with its holomorphic pointwise conjugate. Every coefficient and hence the full family is constant. No finite-dimensional, bounded, compact, trace-class, or `J^2=-1` premise is needed; the argument applies pointwise on any common unbounded-operator domain.

**Survivor / still open**

Same-fibre Kramers doubling cannot produce a nonconstant Xi determinant in the bounded or fixed-domain unbounded holomorphic categories. Only parameter-reversing covariance, genuinely parameter-dependent domains, or a different non-pointwise source law survive; parameter reversal alone permits simple off-real zeros.

The literal `etaPair` has a simple zero at `1+2πi/log 2`, where zeta is nonzero.

`not_exists_analytic_etaPair_squareRoot_rightHalf` excludes a global analytic
square root of this paired source on `Re s>1/2`. The obstruction is an auxiliary
filter zero, not an off-line Xi zero or a disproof of the dyadic-root route.

**Survivor / still open**

simple-zero and limit statements are in Lean source. Producing compatible roots with

<a id="no-go-060"></a>

### symplectic doubling, universal even multiplicity, or quadratic-symbol orientation

**Closed result**

Doubling any nondegenerate symmetric `K`-self-adjoint block produces a nondegenerate alternating form for which `K⊕K` is self-adjoint, so doubled symplectic realization is free. Every skew pairing valued in `{±1}` is symmetric. Xi has a critical-line zero of odd analytic order above every height. Fixed theta root blocks are cyclic and cannot themselves be symplectically self-adjoint; reflected-pair quarter-turns anticommute with the theta operator, and their even dimension is tautological. Clifford successor doubling doubles dimension and cannot remain an exact model of one unchanged Xi multiplicity. The terminal theta character equals `-1` exactly at even multiplicity, so its universal right-half sign is exactly `RHLadder 1`. Leading-Xi-jet reflection parity times that character is identically `-1`, making every coefficient-weighted terminal current reflection-odd regardless of multiplicity.

**Survivor / still open**

A structure presenting the exact Xi divisor cannot force every multiplicity even. Determinant-copy, reflected-pair parity, and leading-jet-weighted oddness are either imposed by duplication, contradicted by Xi, or algebraically nonselective. Surviving first-rung interface is a source law fixing the unsquared terminal theta character without absorbing it into coefficient parity, followed by a cofinal multiplicity ladder or independent local-normality/zero-exclusion theorem.

[Back to family](#roots) · [Original evidence and surrounding qualifications](rh-frontier-reference-2026-09-12.md#source-no-go-060)

<a id="no-go-339"></a>

### one-point Cauchy marginal as horizontal-displacement detector

**Closed result**

A Poisson-smoothed periodic picket fence of growing atom mass has a bounded one-point statistic
for every positive smoothing width, yet its law converges to the standard Cauchy law while every
atom remains horizontally displaced. Thus convergence of the critical-line logarithmic
derivative's one-point marginal to Cauchy does not, by itself, imply vanishing horizontal
displacement. Boole-tail and Hilbert-pair variants recover at most the same first horizontal
moment.

**Survivor / still open**

For zeta, Fujii-type clustering control excludes this abstract counterexample, but then any
converse still needs that cluster bound and remains a receiver for the Littlewood first moment.
The exact cotangent law, Boole identity and weak Cauchy limit survive as formal infrastructure.

Evidence: [picket-fence Cauchy law](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/PoissonSmoothedPicketFenceCauchyLaw.lean), [audit](cauchy-marginal-horizontal-displacement-blindness.md).

<a id="no-go-371"></a>

### MPLF bounded forward raw-jet recovery

**Closed result**

Forward raw prime jets acquire the multiplier `p^(-a)`. Recovering the Meixner root
half-density at time `theta` requires gain `sqrt(w_theta(p)) p^a`, which is unbounded over primes
whenever `theta < 2a`. No bounded diagonal bridge, nor any bounded operator recovering every
marked prime column into unit-norm channels, can perform this recovery. Along every positive root
time sequence tending to zero, the obstruction occurs for every fixed positive forward time.

**Survivor / still open**

Packet-uniform finite Gram bounds along such a null sequence are exactly the critical-line
condition. MPLF must derive them from a differently normalized or indefinite source transport;
bounded columnwise forward recovery is terminal.

Evidence: [forward-bridge no-go](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/MeixnerRootPrimeLaplaceForwardBridgeNoGo.lean), [cofinal Gram classification](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/MeixnerRootPrimeLaplaceCofinalGramClassification.lean).

<a id="no-go-465"></a>

### Uniform local contraction or mixed-cone SOS as free-orbit certificate

**Closed result**

The uniform-certificate card closes two scoped lanes. Lean proves the normalized divisor influence rows sum exactly to `1`, so strict Dobrushin contraction has zero margin; source parity moments amplify rather than contract at a fixed aperture. A separate Lean cone witness blocks semidefinite representability of the mixed real/imaginary reflection cone; identifying the proposed dual certificate with this SOS cone is **paper-only**.

**Survivor / still open**

Exact local prime factorization survives, and the stochastic divisor kernel points to **recurrence**, not contraction. Pure real and pure imaginary frequency cones admit square certificates; the mixed cone is the obstruction. A source-derived recurrent/index or non-SOS certificate remains unproved.

Evidence: [program and status](uniform-certificate-program.md), [stochastic influence](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/ZeroInfluenceStochastic.lean), [cone obstruction](../../../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/ConePositivstellensatzFailure.lean).

### finite nilpotent determinant detector

**Closed result**

For every finite nilpotent matrix, linear endomorphism or continuous endomorphism `N`,

this blindness across Jordan, BRST, Fock, Lax, Wronskian, completion-detector and principal-symbol
nilpotent directions. Ordinary finite determinant cannot detect any of them.

**Survivor / still open**

The exact determinant-one classification and odd/nonzero determinant law for integral `I-2T`
survive. Determinant routes must introduce nonnilpotent or infinite-dimensional spectral data and
prove source occurrence; no approximation-limit conclusion follows from these finite identities.

continuation correction to `-1`, while both unnormalized terms escape and their sum tends

chart recover exactly the same finite zeta continuation. On every strict-left point, the
leading relative-phase QFI has the same limit `4`, so this statistic is not zero-selective.

compensated limit, and the finite continuation remains a valid approximation. Any viable
QFI or phase construction needs subleading, source-sensitive information; leading escape,
chart inversion and circle-filling heuristics are terminal.

<a id="no-go-019"></a>

### continuous Beurling regularity or thin-deletion asymptotics

**Closed result**

One fixed continuous positive Euler pair has counting remainder quadratic in `log x`, hence `o(x^epsilon)` for every `epsilon>0`, and a nonreal zero at `2/3+i`. Geometric-lattice systems have no power saving. Convergent modifying Euler products are zero-free only inside their convergence region. The exact finite deletion sieve is inclusion--exclusion over squarefree deleted-prime products and supplies no cancellation exponent; an assumed exponential boundary deficit cannot be absorbed by any finite-order polynomial lower bound.

**Survivor / still open**

Continuous counting regularity alone is terminal as an orientation principle. Discrete generalized primes, functional equation, boundary continuation, and source-specific squarefree cancellation remain outside these theorems. Thin deletion needs independent arithmetic density/deficit bounds and continuation; logarithmic divergence does not imply the exponential-deficit hypothesis.

<a id="no-go-002"></a>

### CTF finite positivity, fixed-tail weight, or perturbative bounded repair

**Closed result**

Compact radial surgery preserves arbitrary finite positivity yet permits a delayed negative square. Raw heat, vanishing geometric damping, and terminal-weight-one or `epsilon_N log N->0` positive averaging inherit the same obstruction. For every bounded measurable weight supported below one, little-`o(t^(-1/2))` decay of the literal weighted Möbius source forces the weight Mellin transform to vanish at every critical zeta zero; one nonzero moment gives a recurring positive critical-scale floor.

**Survivor / still open**

Harmonic charge is removable, but every successful bounded array needs a persistent correction.
